### [173] [Built-in Selection Bias in Proportional Hazards Models with Omitted Covariates: Simulation Evidence and Alternative Approaches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19982)
*Ayoub Bifenzi,Helene Jacqmin-Gadda*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文探讨了Cox比例风险模型中的风险比(HR)因不可折叠性(non-collapsibility)导致的固有选择偏差问题。即使是在随机对照试验中，如果存在与治疗分配独立但影响结局的未测量协变量（即未观测异质性），标准PH模型估计的治疗HR也会产生偏倚。文章通过模拟评估了不同未观测异质性场景下的偏倚程度，并比较了frailty模型、AFT模型、Kaplan-Meier曲线等替代方法的稳健性，最后用RTOG 9202试验数据进行了实证说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风险比(HR)是生存分析中最常用的治疗效应度量，但由于其不可折叠性——即HR以"存活至各时间点"为条件，导致在存在未测量异质性时产生内置选择偏差(built-in selection bias)。这一问题即使在随机对照试验中也无法避免，因为随机化只保证基线协变量与治疗分配独立，但未测量预后因素仍会导致随时间推移的存活者组成在组间系统性不同。作者旨在系统地揭示这一被低估的问题，并提供实用的替代分析方法。

Method: 首先回顾文献中关于未观测异质性导致HR偏倚的理论机制；其次通过模拟研究，系统评估半参数Cox PH模型和参数PH模型在不同未测量异质性场景（如不同异质性强度、不同分布形式）下的偏倚程度；随后将标准模型与四类替代方法进行比较：(1) frailty模型估计的HR、(2) 加速失效时间(AFT)模型的回归参数、(3) 基于Kaplan-Meier曲线的非参数生存差异估计、(4) 基于含时变效应Cox模型的生存差异估计；最后以RTOG 9202随机对照试验数据进行实证应用。

Result: 模拟结果表明，在存在未测量异质性时，标准Cox PH模型和参数PH模型估计的治疗HR均会产生偏倚，且偏倚程度随异质性强度增大而加剧。相比之下，frailty模型、AFT模型、以及基于Kaplan-Meier曲线或时变效应Cox模型的生存差异估计在应对未观测异质性方面表现更为稳健。RTOG 9202试验数据的实际应用进一步验证了这些替代方法在实践中的相关性和重要性。

Conclusion: 由于HR的不可折叠性，标准Cox PH模型在存在未测量异质性时会产生系统性偏倚，即使随机化也无法消除。研究者应当意识到这一局限性，并在分析中考虑使用frailty模型、AFT模型或直接估计生存差异等替代方法，以获得更可靠的治疗效应估计。

Abstract: In time-to-event analysis, the hazard ratio (HR) derived from the Cox proportional hazards (PH) model is the most commonly used and widely reported measure for assessing treatment effects. However, hazard ratios are non-collapsible due to their inherent conditioning on survival up to each time point. As a result, they are subject to built-in selection bias in the presence of unmeasured heterogeneity arising from omitted important covariates, even when these covariates are independent of the main exposure at baseline, as is the case in randomized controlled trials. This article aims to provide an overview of key findings from the literature on how unobserved heterogeneity, due to omitted covariates that affect the outcome, can bias the estimation of the treatment hazard ratio in standard proportional hazards models, even in randomized trials where treatment is assigned independently of such covariates. Through simulations, we evaluate the extent of bias in the semi-parametric Cox PH model and parametric PH model under various scenarios of unmeasured heterogeneity. We then compare these standard models to alternative approaches that either account for this issue or are considered robust to it. These alternatives include the hazard ratio estimated from frailty models, regression parameters from an Accelerated Failure Time (AFT) model, and survival differences between treatment groups estimated nonparametrically using Kaplan-Meier curves or based on a Cox model with time-dependent effect of the exposure. We illustrate the practical relevance of the explored alternatives through a real data application to a randomized controlled trial from the Radiation Therapy Oncology Group (RTOG 9202).

</details>


### [174] [A minimum-risk and cost-efficient two-sample sequential testing framework for the shifted exponential models with application to precipitation data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20069)
*Ashwani Rajput,Neeraj Joshi*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文针对两个平移指数分布模型的位置参数比较问题，提出了一种双重序贯抽样框架。该方法在控制第一类错误概率的同时，最小化包含第二类错误概率和抽样成本的损失函数，解决了因尺度参数未知且不等导致固定样本设计无法达到目标检验精度的问题，并证明了其具有一阶、二阶效率及二阶风险效率等优良渐近性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在比较两个平移指数分布的位置参数时，最优固定样本量的表达式依赖于未知的尺度参数，导致实践中固定样本设计无法保证所需的检验精度（第一类错误和第二类错误控制）。因此需要一种能够处理尺度参数未知且不等情形的自适应抽样方法。

Method: 提出双重序贯抽样（double sequential sampling）假设检验框架：通过控制第一类错误概率在预设水平，同时最小化一个综合了第二类错误概率与抽样成本的损失函数，推导最优停止规则。该方法不需要预先知道尺度参数，在序贯过程中逐步获取信息以决定何时停止抽样并做出决策。

Result: 理论证明该方法具有一阶效率（first-order efficiency）、二阶效率（second-order efficiency）和二阶风险效率（second-order risk efficiency）。大量模拟研究和针对气象站强降水事件的实际数据应用验证了该方法的有效性和实用性。

Conclusion: 双重序贯抽样方法有效解决了平移指数分布下位置参数比较中尺度参数未知且不等带来的困难，在控制检验误差的同时优化了抽样成本，兼具理论优良性和实际可操作性。

Abstract: This paper investigates the problem of comparing the location parameters of two shifted exponential models through a novel double sequential sampling framework. The proposed hypothesis testing procedure is developed by controlling the type I error probability at a preassigned level while minimizing a loss function that incorporates both the type II error probability and the associated sampling cost. The corresponding optimal fixed-sample-size expressions are shown to depend on unknown scale parameters, rendering the desired testing accuracies unattainable in practice under fixed-sample designs. To overcome this difficulty, a double sequential sampling procedure is proposed to test the difference between location parameters when the scale parameters are unknown and unequal. The proposed methodology is shown to possess desirable asymptotic properties, including first-order efficiency, second-order efficiency, and second-order risk efficiency. Extensive simulation studies and a real-data application that involves heavy precipitation episodes at meteorological stations demonstrate the practical effectiveness and applicability of the proposed procedure.

</details>


### [175] [Community detection in small-sample ordinal regimes: A benchmarking framework for Delphi data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20114)
*Yuri Calleo,Simone Di Zio,Fabrizio Maturo*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 针对德尔菲法中高维度问卷项目与有限专家样本量导致的秩亏问题，本文提出从变量中心协方差模型转向网络中心连通性模型，利用加权图拓扑上的社区发现算法识别潜在主题结构，并通过合成数据基准系统验证了拓扑方法在小样本高维场景下的稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 德尔菲共识数据的统计建模面临关键瓶颈：问卷项目维度高而专家面板样本量有限，导致传统潜变量模型（如PCA）结构不稳定且易过拟合。这一方法学空白亟需新的降维策略来应对高维低样本量（HDLSS）体制下的谱不稳定性与秩亏问题。

Method: 提出从变量中心协方差模型向网络中心连通性模型的范式转换。具体而言：(1) 将项目相关性映射到加权图拓扑上；(2) 运用社区发现算法识别潜在主题结构；(3) 构建基于合成数据的仿真基准，系统评估结构密度、信息流和谱划分三种拓扑方法的稳健性；(4) 合成数据设计模拟了共识数据的病理条件，包括序数量表和系统噪声。

Result: 研究表明，专家判断间的共线性——传统上被视为需正则化的统计冗余——可被有效重新解读为凝聚力的拓扑信号。该方法在标准因子分析失效的小样本体制下仍能保持结构稳定性和心理测量一致性。

Conclusion: 该框架为研究者提供了一种结构化、自动化的降维程序，在传统因子分析崩溃的小样本高维条件下确保了结构稳定性与心理测量一致性，为德尔菲法的方法论提供了重要补充。

Abstract: The statistical modeling of consensus in Delphi data faces a critical bottleneck: the high dimensionality of questionnaire items relative to the limited sample size of expert panels. This rank deficiency leads traditional latent variable models, such as Principal Component Analysis, to be structurally unstable and prone to overfitting. Addressing this methodological gap, this study proposes a transition from variable-centric covariance models to network-centric connectivity models. By mapping item correlations onto a weighted graph topology, we present a simulation-based benchmark that utilizes community detection algorithms to identify latent thematic structures, effectively addressing the spectral instability and rank deficiency typical of high-dimensional, low-sample-size regimes. The research systematically evaluates the robustness of topological approaches based on structural density, information flow, and spectral partitioning against synthetic datasets designed to replicate the pathological conditions of consensus data, including ordinal scales and systemic noise. The central methodological contribution lies in demonstrating that collinearity among expert judgments - traditionally treated as statistical redundancy to be regularized - can be effectively reinterpreted as a topological signal of cohesion. This framework provides researchers with a structured and automated procedure for dimensionality reduction, ensuring structural stability and psychometric consistency even in small-sample regimes where standard factor analysis breaks down.

</details>


### [176] [A case study of causal mediation using Bayesian nonparametrics and semiparametric corrections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20148)
*Yuhua Zhang,Michael J. Daniels*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出一种截断的富集狄利克雷过程混合模型（EDPM），用于在存在治疗后混杂因素的情况下估计因果中介分析中的自然直接效应（NDE）和自然间接效应（NIE），并通过高效的后验校正步骤获得具有良好频率性质的贝叶斯估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在因果中介分析中，存在治疗后混杂因素时，传统方法难以可靠地估计自然直接和间接效应。贝叶斯非参数模型虽然能灵活建模复杂联合分布，但从中提取特定因果估计量（如NDE/NIE）并保证良好的频率性质（如正确的覆盖率）是一个关键难题。

Method: 采用截断的富集狄利克雷过程混合模型（truncated EDPM）进行贝叶斯非参数建模；引入一种高效的簇重分配Metropolis-Hastings算法以改善分块Gibbs采样的混合性能；基于高效影响函数实施一步后验校正，使特定因果估计量获得可靠的区间估计和频率性质。

Result: 通过模拟研究验证了方法的性能，并在一项体重管理临床试验中成功应用于评估因果中介效应。

Conclusion: 所提出的贝叶斯非参数方法结合后验校正策略，能够在存在治疗后混杂因素的复杂场景下，同时利用贝叶斯非参数模型的灵活性和频率学派估计的优良性质，实现对NDE和NIE的可靠推断。

Abstract: We propose a Bayesian nonparametric approach using a truncated Enriched Dirichlet Process mixture (EDPM) model to estimate natural direct (NDE) and indirect (NIE) effects in causal mediation analyses in the presence of post-treatment confounders. We introduce an efficient cluster reallocation Metropolis-Hasting algorithm to improve mixing in the blocked Gibbs sampler. We implement a one-step posterior correction based on the efficient influence function for our setting. This post-processing step solves a critical problem in Bayesian nonparametrics: how to obtain reliable estimates and posteriors for a specific causal estimand of interest (the NDE and NIE) with excellent frequentist properties, such as correct coverage, from a model designed for complex joint distributions. We conduct simulation studies to assess our method's performance and apply it to evaluate causal mediation effects in a weight management clinical trial.

</details>


### [177] [Analysis of uncertain fixed-effects model for Latin square designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20226)
*Yaru Cheng,Zhiming Li*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文针对缺乏频率稳定性的不确定数据，基于不确定测度建立了拉丁方设计的不确定固定效应模型，提出了三种带有不确定性的估计方法及其置信区间，并通过数值模拟和实际教育数据验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典固定效应模型仅适用于精确的实验数据，而实验设计中常出现缺乏频率稳定性的不确定数据，因此需要建立能够处理此类不确定数据的固定效应模型。

Method: 基于不确定测度理论，为拉丁方设计建立不确定固定效应模型；提出三种不确定性估计方法用于估计处理效应和区组效应，并构建其置信区间；设计不确定齐性检验和共同检验以评估处理效应的显著性。

Result: 通过数值模拟，从偏差、均方误差、平均绝对误差、总体标准差、覆盖概率和平均区间长度六个指标对三种估计方法进行了比较。多个示例展示了估计和假设检验的流程，并在真实教育数据上验证了模型的实用价值。

Conclusion: 所提出的不确定固定效应模型能够有效处理拉丁方设计中的不确定数据，具有实际应用价值，为实验设计中非精确数据的分析提供了新工具。

Abstract: Uncertain data without frequency stability often arises in experimental design. Classical fixed-effects models can only analyze precise experimental data. Based on an uncertain measure, this paper establishes uncertain fixed-effect models for Latin-square designs. First, we propose three methods with uncertainty to estimate the treatment and blocked effects and construct their confidence intervals. Then, uncertain homogeneity and common tests are conducted to assess the significance of treatment effects. In the numerical simulations, the three estimation methods are compared based on bias, mean squared error, mean absolute error, overall standard deviation, coverage probability, and average interval length. Several examples are given to illustrate the process of estimation and hypothesis. Finally, the uncertain fixed-effects model is applied to real education data, demonstrating its practical value.

</details>


### [178] [Anchors Away: Navigating Unanchored Indirect Comparisons with Multilevel Unanchored Meta-Regression (ML-UMR)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20341)
*Conor Chandler,Jack Ishak*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: ML-UMR是一种贝叶斯回归框架，可在无共同参照组的非锚定间接比较中联合建模个体数据与聚合数据，估计多治疗、多研究和多目标人群的治疗效应，并显式区分识别效应与人群外推所需的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当随机对照证据缺失时，HTA中越来越多地使用单臂研究或完全断联证据进行非锚定间接比较。现有方法（MAIC、STC）主要限于成对比较，且通常估计的是比较研究人群而非决策相关人群中的边际效应，存在明显局限。

Method: 提出多层次非锚定元回归（ML-UMR），将多层次网络元回归（ML-NMR）扩展到非锚定场景。通过统一似然函数联合建模个体层面和聚合层面数据，同时利用个体患者数据（IPD）和聚合数据（AgD），实现多治疗、多研究及多目标人群的边际与条件效应估计。

Result: 模拟研究中，ML-UMR对比较人群效应产生低偏倚和名义覆盖率。人群外推的可移植性严重依赖识别假设：当共享预后因子假设（SPFA）被违反且存在强效应修饰时出现偏倚；但纳入亚组信息后可恢复近乎无偏的估计和名义覆盖率。

Conclusion: ML-UMR提供了一个统一框架，使非锚定比较中所需的强假设（条件可交换性、模型正确设定、跨治疗假设如SPFA）更加显式化，并便于进行敏感性分析。但该方法并不能降低这些假设的强度，有效推断仍依赖这些通常不可验证的假设。

Abstract: Unanchored indirect treatment comparisons using single-arm studies or disconnected evidence are increasingly used in health technology assessment (HTA) when randomized evidence is unavailable. Existing methods, including matching-adjusted indirect comparison (MAIC) and simulated treatment comparison (STC), are generally limited to pairwise settings and typically estimate marginal effects in the comparator study population, which may differ from the decision-relevant population.
  We propose multilevel unanchored meta-regression (ML-UMR), a Bayesian regression framework for synthesizing individual patient data and aggregate data from fully disconnected evidence. ML-UMR extends multilevel network meta-regression (ML-NMR) to unanchored settings by jointly modeling individual- and aggregate-level data within a unified likelihood, enabling estimation of treatment-specific outcomes and both marginal and conditional effects across multiple treatments, studies, and target populations.
  ML-UMR distinguishes assumptions required to identify treatment effects from those required to transport results to target populations. As with all unanchored comparisons, valid inference relies on strong and often unverifiable assumptions, including conditional exchangeability, correct specification of the outcome model, and cross-treatment assumptions (e.g., shared prognostic factor assumption (SPFA)). ML-UMR does not lessen these requirements but makes them explicit within a unified framework and facilitates sensitivity analyses.
  In simulation studies, ML-UMR produced low bias and nominal coverage for comparator-population effects. Transportability to alternative populations depended critically on identifying assumptions: violations of SPFA led to bias under strong effect modification, whereas incorporating subgroup information restored near-unbiased estimation and nominal coverage.

</details>


### [179] [Flexible modeling of bimodal distributions via skewed-$t$ mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20406)
*Marco Bee,Flavio Santi*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于位置-尺度偏态t分布的混合模型，用于拟合双峰、偏态和重尾数据，通过EM算法进行极大似然估计，并应用于S&P 500数据验证了美股市场的双峰特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实数据常呈现双峰、偏态和重尾等复杂特征，单一的对称分布无法有效捕捉这些特征。现有混合模型在灵活性和估计精度方面仍有改进空间，需要一种既能处理偏态和重尾、又易于推广到其他对称分布混合的建模框架。

Method: 采用Fernández和Steel (1998)提出的偏态t分布构建位置-尺度混合模型(location-scale mixture)。首先研究混合分布的统计性质，然后通过EM算法实现极大似然估计，并提出似然比检验来检验各混合成分是否存在偏度（原假设为无偏度）。通过仿真对比所提模型与混合g-and-h分布模型在正确设定和误设定框架下的表现。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提模型在正确设定下具有优异的估计精度，在误设定框架下也展现出良好的建模能力。将模型应用于S&P 500指数数据，证实了其分布的双峰性，意味着美股市场历史上倾向于处于熊市或牛市两种状态，而非围绕基本面价值波动。

Conclusion: 基于Fernández-Steel偏态t分布的混合模型能够有效拟合具有双峰、偏态和重尾特征的数据，估计精度和建模灵活性均优于混合g-and-h分布模型，且模型框架易于推广至其他对称分布的混合。

Abstract: We propose a mixture of location-scale skewed-$t$ distributions to fit bimodal, skewed and heavy-tailed data. In particular, the mixture is based on the skewed-$t$ distribution by Fernández and Steel (1998), so that the model-building procedure can be easily extended to mixtures of other symmetric distributions. After studying the properties of the mixture, we develop a maximum likelihood estimation approach via the EM algorithm and a likelihood ratio test of the null hypothesis of no skewness in any given component. A simulation-based comparison to a recently proposed mixture of g-and-h distributions suggests that the performance of the proposed model is excellent, in terms of both estimation precision in well-specified setups and modeling capability in mis-specified frameworks. Fitting the model to the Standard & Poor's 500 distortion allows us to confirm the bimodality of its distribution, with the implication that the US stock market has historically been in bearish or bullish conditions, rather than near its fundamental value.

</details>


### [180] [Hypergraph Variable Selection with False Discovery Rate Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20514)
*Sarah Organ,Toby Kenney,Hong Gu*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于超图的变量选择方法，通过定义在预测变量集合上的广义错误发现率，在保持严格FDR控制的同时，提高了复杂依赖结构下的统计功效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统控制错误发现率（FDR）的变量选择方法在预测变量存在复杂依赖结构时常常损失统计功效。此前已有研究表明，层次聚类分组选择可缓解该问题，但当相关性结构不够层次化时效果不佳，因此需要一种能处理更广泛依赖结构的灵活方法。

Method: 作者引入了一种定义在预测变量集合上的广义错误发现率（generalized FDR）框架，并提出基于超图（hypergraph）的选择方法。超图结构允许预测变量形成重叠的集合，从而更灵活地捕捉复杂的相关性模式。

Result: 该方法在多种实验设置下均表现出更高的统计功效（power），并且能够保持严格的错误发现率控制。

Conclusion: 基于超图的广义FDR框架有效解决了复杂依赖结构下变量选择功效不足的问题，为相关领域的变量选择提供了一种更具普适性和鲁棒性的方法论。

Abstract: Variable selection methods that control the false discovery rate often lose power when predictors exhibit complex dependence structures. We previously showed that selecting hierarchically clustered groups of predictors can mitigate this issue while maintaining false discovery rate control. When correlations are less structured, however, overlapping predictor sets may be more effective. We introduce a generalized false discovery rate for hypotheses defined on sets of predictors and propose a hypergraph-based selection method. This approach achieves higher power across diverse settings while preserving rigorous false discovery rate control.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [181] [Deontic Policies for Runtime Governance of Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19464)
*Anupam Joshi,Tim Finin,Karuna Pande Joshi,Lalana Kagal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 AgenticRei 框架，基于道义逻辑和 OWL 本体语言，解决 LLM 驱动的自主 AI 代理在企业治理中超越传统"允许/禁止"的合规需求，包括义务管理、豁免、策略冲突解决和领域本体推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 LLM 驱动的自主 AI 代理系统引入了新的安全、隐私和合规挑战。代理不仅能调用工具、操作数据、安装软件，还能跨组织边界协调，传统的认证与访问控制已不足以约束其行为。现有策略引擎（如 XACML、Rego、Cedar）仅覆盖"允许/禁止"子集，无法处理义务生命周期管理、元策略冲突解决、特定场景下的义务豁免，以及医疗、网络安全、数据隐私等领域的本体层级推理。因此需要一个完整的治理框架来填补这一空白。

Method: 作者提出 AgenticRei，基于 Rei 框架构建道义策略语言，以 OWL（Web 本体语言）表达策略，并在 LLM 之外通过高性能逻辑引擎进行运行时评估。该流水线同时治理代理的工具调用和代理间消息通信。策略模型涵盖：允许/禁止约束、义务（obligations）及其生命周期管理、豁免（dispensations）机制、策略冲突的元策略解决，以及基于领域本体类层级的推理。

Result: 通过示例展示，道义策略能够捕捉当前生产级策略引擎几乎无法表达的安全与隐私治理约束。该方法可与行业标准框架（如 A2A 协议）自然组合。

Conclusion: AgenticRei 填补了当前策略引擎在 AI 代理治理中的关键空白，将道义逻辑与语义网技术结合，为自主代理提供了超越访问控制的全面企业治理能力，且具备与工业标准框架的兼容性。

Abstract: Autonomous agentic AI systems driven by Large Language Models (LLMs) introduce a new class of security, privacy, and compliance challenges: an agent that can invoke tools, manipulate data, install software, and coordinate with peer agents across organizational boundaries must be constrained not just by authentication and access control, but by the full structure of enterprise governance. This includes specifying what agents are permitted and prohibited from doing, what they areobliged to do after certain actions (e.g., notify the CISO), under what conditions a standing obligation may be waived, and which rules take precedence when policies conflict. This governance problem exceeds what current policy engines provide. Systems such as XACML, Rego, and Cedar address only the permit/prohibit subset of this governance structure. They do not provide obligation lifecycle management, meta-policy conflict resolution, dispensations that waive obligations in specific circumstances, and ontological reasoning over domain class hierarchies commonly found in applications such as healthcare, cybersecurity, or data privacy. We propose AgenticRei, which realizes key governance requirements such as obligations, dispensations, policy conflict resolutions, and reasoning over policies, as well as the basic permit/prohibit constraints. We use a deontic policy language built on the Rei framework, expressed as OWL (Web Ontology Language) and evaluated at runtime by a high-performance logic engine entirely outside the LLM. The same pipeline governs both tool invocations by the agent and agent-to-agent messages. We show through examples that deontic policies capture governance constraints around security and privacy that mostly cannot be expressed in current production engines. Our approach composes naturally with industry-standard frameworks like A2AS.

</details>


### [182] [Measuring Curriculum Alignment across Topical Coverage, Competency, and Cognitive Depth: A Longitudinal Framework Applied to CS2013 and CS2023](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19469)
*Sherzod Turaev,Mary John,Saja Aldabet,Mamoun Awad,Nazar Zaki,Khaled Shuaib*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种人机协同流水线方法，用于量化衡量计算机科学本科项目对国际课程指南（CS2013和CS2023）的覆盖程度。通过语义检索生成候选匹配，再由人工确认，发现该项目对两个指南的知识单元覆盖率均约50%，十年间基本稳定；但在认知深度上，CS2023的达标率（76%）明显低于CS2013（95%），反映了新标准提高了期望。该方法可复用，并识别出持久的结构性差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CS本科项目缺乏可靠、可复现的方法来衡量其对国际课程指南的覆盖程度及其随指南更新的变化。现有评估手段无法系统追踪项目与外部知识体系的对齐情况，尤其是指南大约每十年修订一次，项目需要一种纵向比较工具。

Method: 设计了一个人机协同流水线：1）将项目课程和指南知识单元表示为结构化语料库；2）通过语义检索生成课程到知识单元的候选匹配（对比了七种检索器，包括倒数排名融合集成和长上下文模型）；3）在明确定义的覆盖标准下，由人工确认匹配结果；4）由独立第二评分者验证（Cohen's κ为0.64和0.69）；5）延伸至能力表述和认知深度分析，并结合ABET标准进行纵向对比。

Result: 该项目覆盖CS2023的49.7%和CS2013的50.9%知识单元，十年间基本恒定。能力表述方面，约88%的已覆盖单元被明确阐明；认知深度方面，CS2023下达标率仅76%，而CS2013下为95%。识别出的持久结构性差距包括：并行与分布式计算、程序设计语言基础、系统基础等。检索器选择显著影响效果：倒数排名融合集成表现最佳，某知名长上下文模型反而不及小型句子模型。

Conclusion: 该流水线为CS项目提供了一种可复用、可复现的课程覆盖评估工具，能够有效区分由标准演变引起的差异与项目自身的结构性差距，为课程改革提供数据驱动的决策依据。

Abstract: Undergraduate computer science is governed by international curricular guidelines revised about once a decade, yet programs lack a reliable, reproducible way to measure how completely they cover the current guidelines and how that coverage shifts when the guidelines are restructured. We address this with a human-in-the-loop pipeline that measures a program's coverage of an external body of knowledge, applied longitudinally to one accredited BSc in Computer Science against Computer Science Curricula 2013 (CS2013) and 2023 (CS2023). The pipeline represents the program and each guideline as structured corpora, generates candidate course-to-knowledge-unit matches by semantic retrieval, and confirms them through human judgment under an explicit coverage definition. Of seven benchmarked retrievers, a reciprocal-rank-fusion ensemble was strongest, and a reputed long-context model underperformed a small sentence model, so retriever choice must be measured. Both maps were validated by an independent second rater (Cohen's kappa 0.64 for CS2023, 0.69 for CS2013). The program covers 49.7% of CS2023 and 50.9% of CS2013 knowledge units, near-constant across a decade. Extending the same retrieve-then-confirm design to competency articulation and cognitive depth shows that the program articulates the competency for ~88% of covered units under each guideline, yet delivers it at the recommended depth for 76% of present units under CS2023 against 95% under CS2013, a gap reflecting the newer guideline's raised expectations, not the program. The longitudinal comparison separates persistent structural gaps (parallel and distributed computing, foundations of programming languages, systems fundamentals), uncovered against both guidelines and ABET, from differences that reflect the standard's evolution. The instrument is reusable and available from the authors on request.

</details>


### [183] [Diffusion Language Models: An Experimental Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19475)
*Thomas Bertolani,Davide Bucciarelli,Leonardo Zini,Marcella Cornia,Lorenzo Baraldi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对现代扩散语言模型（DLMs）进行了系统性实验分析，评估了8种先进DLMs在推理、编码、翻译等8个基准上的表现，重点考察生成质量与计算效率之间的权衡，并分析了去噪步数、上下文长度等关键推理时因素的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型作为自回归语言模型的替代范式，通过迭代去噪生成文本，具备并行优化的潜力。然而，各类扩散架构由于评估协议、数据集、推理预算和生成超参数的不一致，导致难以公平比较其能力和权衡关系。本文旨在填补这一系统性对比分析的空白。

Method: 作者选取8种最先进的DLMs，在涵盖推理、编码、翻译、知识问答和结构化问题求解的8个基准上进行统一评估；同时显式考量生成质量（quality）和计算效率（efficiency）。此外，通过控制变量实验分析推理时关键因素（去噪步数、上下文长度、块大小、并行去掩码策略），并在相同训练条件下对比小规模模型以进行受控比较。

Result: 研究表明，DLMs的行为受生成时设计选择强烈影响，不同任务、架构和推理预算下呈现明显的性能-效率权衡。扩散语言模型在不同场景中展现出各自的优势与局限。

Conclusion: DLMs在性能和计算效率之间存在本质的权衡，生成阶段的超参数选择至关重要。本文为理解和部署当代扩散语言模型提供了实用的 insights，有助于研究者根据具体应用需求做出合适的架构与推理策略选择。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have revolutionized language modeling through autoregressive generation, enabling strong performance across a wide range of tasks. Recently, Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) have emerged as an alternative paradigm that generates text through iterative denoising rather than next-token prediction, allowing parallel refinement of entire sequences. While numerous diffusion-based architectures have been proposed, differences in evaluation protocols, datasets, inference budgets, and generation hyperparameters make it difficult to compare their capabilities and understand the trade-offs they offer. In this work, we present a systematic experimental analysis of modern DLMs. Specifically, we evaluate eight state-of-the-art DLMs across eight benchmarks spanning reasoning, coding, translation, knowledge, and structured problem solving, while explicitly considering both generation quality and computational efficiency. Beyond downstream evaluation, we analyze the impact of key inference-time factors, including denoising steps, context length, block size, and parallel unmasking strategies, and complement large-scale experiments with controlled comparisons of smaller models trained under identical conditions. Our analysis highlights the strengths and limitations of diffusion-based language modeling across different tasks, architectures, and inference budgets. We show that the behavior of DLMs is strongly influenced by generation-time design choices, leading to distinct trade-offs between performance and computational efficiency. Overall, our study provides practical insights into the capabilities and deployment characteristics of contemporary DLMs.

</details>


### [184] [Hidden Anchors in Multi-Agent LLM Deliberation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19494)
*Apurba Pokharel,Ram Dantu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将多智能体LLM审议建模为闭环动力系统，其中每个智能体持有一个隐藏的内部信念（锚点），持续牵引其意见。该锚点可从审议过程恢复，并能解释传统共识规则无法解释的现象——智能体对正确答案的置信度可超越初始信念的凸包。在三种开源模型家族上的实验表明，锚点影响力普遍存在，但仅当锚点远离初始意见时，审议才会逃逸凸包。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体LLM审议研究缺乏对其工作机制的形式化建模，而人类决策既受群体影响（从众效应），又受内在信念驱动。传统意见动力学模型（如DeGroot、Friedkin-Johnsen）仅捕捉群体影响，忽略了内在信念的持续牵引作用。

Method: 将多智能体审议建模为闭环动力系统：每个智能体携带隐藏内在信念（锚点），该锚点独立于邻居意见，持续牵引其公开意见。锚点可从审议过程的公开意见序列中恢复。通过检验恢复的锚点能否泛化到留存的运行数据，判断模型是否真正由锚点驱动。

Result: 在三类开源模型家族中，锚点影响力普遍存在且强度相近，但锚点位置各异。仅当锚点远离初始意见时，审议才会突破初始信念的凸包，此时需要完整的闭环模型来描述。锚点泛化检验呈现出谱系特征，而非全有或全无。

Conclusion: 内在锚点机制有效解释了多智能体LLM审议为何能超越初始信念空间，且提供了模型驱动力的可检验标准。该闭环动力系统框架为理解和分析LLM审议行为提供了形式化工具。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM deliberation, where agents exchange and revise answers over several rounds, is increasingly used to improve reasoning and accuracy, yet how and why it works is rarely modelled. Such deliberation mirrors how humans reach decisions. As social animals we are pulled both by the group, the herd effect that classical opinion-dynamics models such as DeGroot and Friedkin--Johnsen capture, and by our own internal belief, which they do not. We model multi-agent deliberation as a closed-loop dynamical system in which each agent carries a hidden internal belief, its anchor, that continually pulls its opinion regardless of its neighbours. We show this anchor can be recovered from the deliberation alone, and that it explains a behaviour classical consensus rules forbid: an agent's confidence in the correct answer can climb past where any agent started, escaping the space (convexhull) formed by the initial beliefs. Checking whether the recovered anchor also predicts held-out runs (generalizes) gives a simple test for when a model is truly driven bysuch an anchor. Across three open-weight model families this is a spectrum, not all-or-nothing. All anchors' influence are about equally strongly, but they differ in where the anchor sits, and only when it sits far from the initial opinions does deliberation escape the hull and need the full closed-loop model.

</details>


### [185] [DeXposure-Claw: An Agentic System for DeFi Risk Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19501)
*Aijie Shu,Bowei Chen,Wenbin Wu,Cathy Yi-Hsuan Chen,Fengxiang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对去中心化金融监管中LLM代理误报率高的问题，本文提出DeXposure-Claw系统，通过图时间序列基础模型进行风险预测，结合确定性监控与置信度门控生成可审计的监管票据，并构建了面向监管者的六维评估基准DeXposure-Bench。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化金融（DeFi）使监管者面临快速演变的网络化信用风险，但通用大语言模型（LLM）代理在该场景下表现不佳：它们会过度解读弱证据并错误建议高风险干预措施，而现有评估方法缺乏与监管目标对齐的误报率衡量手段。

Method: 提出DeXposure-Claw系统，分三步：① DeXposure-FM——图时间序列基础模型，预测未来风险暴露网络；② 确定性监控器和压力情景将预测转化为带类型的警报、归因信号和情景证据；③ 数据健康与置信度门控机制约束升级路径，确保最终输出的监管票据附带推理依据且可审计。同时构建DeXposure-Bench六维评估框架，其决策轴以监管对齐的绝对损失真值和显式误干预率对票据进行评分。

Result: 在五年周频真实数据上的实验全面支持所提系统的有效性。

Conclusion: DeXposure-Claw通过结构化证据路由和置信度门控，有效抑制了LLM代理的误报问题，为DeFi监管提供了可审计、可度量的代理监督方案。

Abstract: Decentralized finance exposes supervisors to fast-moving, networked credit risks. General-purpose LLM agents fit this setting poorly: they over-read weak evidence and recommend high-stakes interventions, while existing evaluations offer no regulator-aligned way to measure the resulting false alarms. We introduce DeXposure-Claw, a forecast-grounded agentic supervision system that routes LLM decisions through structured evidence: (1) DeXposure-FM, a graph time-series foundation model, forecasts future exposure networks; (2) deterministic monitors and stress scenarios then turn those forecasts into typed alerts, attribution signals, and scenario evidence; and (3) data-health and confidence gates constrain escalation before DeXposure-Claw emits auditable supervisory tickets with rationales. We further develop DeXposure-Bench, a six-axis evaluation harness, whose decision axis scores tickets against a regulator-aligned absolute-loss ground truth and an explicit false-intervention rate. Experiments on five years of weekly real data fully support our system. Code is at https://github.com/EVIEHub/DeXposure-Claw.

</details>


### [186] [LLM Doesn't Know What It Doesn't Know: Detecting Epistemic Blind Spots via Cross-Model Attribution Divergence on Clinical Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19509)
*Akshat Dasula,Prasanna Desikan,Jaideep Srivastava*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了大语言模型（LLMs）在结构化临床数据任务中是否具备认知自身知识边界的能力。通过归因分歧分析，对比Qwen 2.5 7B与XGBoost，发现LLM的口头置信度在认知上是空洞的，存在逆难度效应；而少量样本示例与SHAP特征证据可产生超加性干预效果；此外，利用归因分歧信号构建的跨模型校准器可将预期校准误差从0.254降至0.080，无需访问模型内部或重复推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs日益被应用于结构化临床数据任务，但其是否能够识别自身知识的局限性（即认知性自我意识）仍未被探索。这一能力对于高风险医疗场景至关重要，因为模型需要知道自己何时"不知道"。

Method: 研究者通过跨模型归因分歧（cross-model attribution divergence）的视角切入，将Qwen 2.5 7B与XGBoost在同一预测任务上进行对比。主要方法包括：分析LLM口头置信度与真实预测质量的关系；比较不同难度样本上LLM与XGBoost的性能差异；引入少量样本示例和SHAP特征证据作为干预手段，以归因分歧分数（ADS）衡量效果；构建跨模型校准器，利用归因分歧信号判断LLM可靠性，无需访问模型内部参数或重复推理。

Result: 四项核心发现：(1) LLM的口头置信度范围恒定在0.856–0.937，与准确率无关，仅受提示格式影响；(2) 逆难度效应：XGBoost 99%正确的样本上LLM准确率降至64.8%，但在XGBoost中等不确定时LLM表现与之持平；(3) 少量样本示例与SHAP特征证据呈正交且超可加性，联合使用可将ADS从1.54降至0.38，准确率从49%提升至75.3%；(4) 跨模型校准器将预期校准误差从0.254降至0.080，提供患者特异性可靠性估计。

Conclusion: LLM在结构化数据任务上面临"冷启动问题"：其口头表达的置信度与真实预测质量脱节，缺乏真正的认知自我意识。通过归因分歧分析和跨模型校准，可以在不访问模型内部、无需重复推理的情况下，为LLM赋予患者特异性的可靠性估计，为走向真正的认知自我意识提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly applied to structured clinical data, yet whether they can recognize the limits of their own knowledge on such tasks remains unexplored. We study this question through the lens of cross-model attribution divergence with the goal of reducing epistemic uncertainty for structured tasks, comparing Qwen 2.5 7B and XGBoost on a prediction task via attribution divergence analysis. We report four findings. First, LLM verbalized confidence is epistemically vacuous, it outputs a near-constant (0.856-0.937) regardless of whether accuracy is 49% or 75.3%, tracking prompt format rather than prediction quality. Second, the LLM exhibits an inverse difficulty effect: accuracy drops to 64.8% when XGBoost is 99% correct, but matches XGBoost (73.8% vs. 73.1%) when it is moderately uncertain. Third, few-shot examples and SHAP-derived feature evidence are orthogonal, super-additive interventions: they reduce the Attribution Disagreement Score (ADS) from 1.54 to 0.38 and improve accuracy from 49% to 75.3% without training. Fourth, a cross-model calibrator that determined LLM reliability using attribution divergence signals reduces expected calibration error from 0.254 to 0.080, replacing uninformative verbalized confidence with patient-specific reliability estimates, without accessing model internals or requiring repeated inference. We frame these findings as a cold start problem for LLMs on structured data and outline a path toward genuine epistemic self-awareness.

</details>


### [187] [REVEAL++: Differentiable Phenotypic Grouping for Vision-Language Retinal Modeling of Alzheimer's Disease Risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19522)
*Ethan Elio Meidinger,Seowung Leem,Zeyun Zhao,Ruogu Fang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将表型相似性建模为连续可学习信号的对比学习框架，用于视网膜图像与临床风险文本的多模态对齐，以预测阿尔茨海默病。相比传统的离散分组方法，该方法通过可微权重函数定义软多正样本关系，实现端到端的跨模态表征学习，在UK Biobank数据上表现优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视网膜-临床多模态对比学习方法（如REVEAL）采用离散表型分组，将相似风险个体硬性归入固定簇，导致分组与表征学习解耦，且刚性监督无法反映疾病风险的谱系特性（从正常到AD的渐变连续过程）。

Method: 提出连续表型结构的对比学习框架：(1) 利用视网膜图像与风险文本的模态内嵌入相似度，构建可微的受试者间相似性权重函数；(2) 通过连续聚合算子定义软多正样本关系，替代硬分组；(3) 引入软目标对比损失函数，端到端联合学习跨模态对齐与表型结构。

Result: 在UK Biobank视网膜影像数据上进行前瞻性AD预测评估，所提框架一致性地优于基于离散分组的对比学习方法和标准视觉-语言基线模型。

Conclusion: 将表型相似性视为可学习的连续信号而非固定分组规则，为基于多模态视网膜与临床数据的大规模神经退行性疾病风险建模提供了更具原则性和鲁棒性的基础。

Abstract: The retina offers a noninvasive window into neurodegenerative disease, capturing subtle structural patterns associated with a risk of future cognitive decline. Vision-language alignment frameworks such as REVEAL have shown that pairing retinal fundus images with structured clinical risk narratives improves early prediction of Alzheimer's disease (AD). A key design choice in these approaches is the use of phenotypic grouping, where individuals with similar risk profiles are treated as multi-positive pairs during contrastive learning. However, existing methods operationalize phenotypic similarity as a discrete construct, relying on hard group assignments that impose rigid supervision and decouple group formation from representation learning. We propose a continuous formulation of phenotypic structure within contrastive learning. Rather than assigning samples to fixed clusters, we model inter-subject similarity as a differentiable weighting function derived from intra-modality embedding similarities in both retinal images and risk profiles. These weights define soft multi-positive relationships through a continuous aggregation operator, enabling graded supervision that reflects the spectrum nature of disease risk. We further introduce a soft-target contrastive objective that jointly learns cross-modal alignment and phenotypic structure in an end-to-end manner. Evaluated on UK Biobank retinal imaging data for incident AD prediction, the proposed framework consistently outperforms discrete group-based contrastive learning and standard vision-language baselines. By treating phenotypic similarity as a learnable, continuous signal rather than a fixed grouping rule, our approach provides a principled and robust foundation for population-scale neurodegenerative risk modeling from multi-modal retinal and clinical data.

</details>


### [188] [Emergent Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19527)
*Martin Kolář*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在线对齐技术，使大语言模型通过“良知”步骤审视自身输出，并利用DPO损失函数引导模型远离不道德输出，从而在无需外部评判模型的情况下实现自对齐。在代码黑客微调场景中，仅通过一个高层内省问题即可实现“涌现对齐”，而非先前工作所展示的“涌现失配”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型在特定微调场景下会出现“涌现失配”现象，产生不符合人类伦理的输出。现有对齐方法通常依赖外部评判模型（弱或强监督者），而本文探索是否能让模型依赖自身冻结副本进行自我审视与纠正，实现无需外部监督的伦理自对齐。

Method: 提出“良知步骤”（conscience step），让模型在输出前对其推理和输出进行自我审视。训练损失函数中引入基于DPO的对齐组件，利用模型自身的冻结副本作为参照，通过对比偏好优化引导模型远离非伦理输出。该方法可覆盖训练、微调、对抗提示和零样本学习等多种应用场景。

Result: 在代码黑客微调场景中，仅通过加入一个高层内省问题，模型即从“涌现失配”转变为“涌现对齐”，成功引导训练走向符合伦理的模型，实证了自对齐的有效性。

Conclusion: LLM借助自身冻结副本和内省机制即可实现伦理自对齐，无需外部评判模型。这为模型对齐提供了一种轻量、在线的可行方案，并证明了“涌现对齐”可以在与“涌现失配”相同的条件下发生。

Abstract: Can Large Language Models (LLMs) discern when their own outputs are misaligned with human ethics? And can they self-correct? We endow an LLM with a conscience step that reviews its own reasoning and outputs, and we extend the training loss with an alignment component using Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) to steer the model away from non-ethical outputs. The result is an online technique to align models in a wide range of applications: training, fine-tuning, adversarial prompting, and zero-shot learning. It does not require a weaker or stronger judge, relying instead on a frozen copy of itself. In previous work, the Emergent Misalignment scenario showed a range of emergent unethical behaviors from fine-tuning the model to hack code. Instead, we empirically show how to achieve Emergent Alignment: a single high-level introspective question steers training toward an ethical model under the same code hacking scenario.

</details>


### [189] [ITNet: A Learnable Integral Transform That Subsumes Convolution, Attention, and Recurrence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19538)
*Ashim Dhor,Rasel Mondal,Pin Yu Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出积分变换网络（ITNet），将卷积、自注意力和循环网络统一为一个可学习的积分变换框架，核心是一个由MLP实现的可学习核函数。通过适当参数化，ITNet可退化为各类经典架构，且在ImageNet-1K、GLUE等多个基准上匹配或超越专用基线，证明单一学习交互机制足以从数据中恢复三大架构家族的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者观察到卷积网络（局部性）、循环网络（序列记忆）和Transformer（内容依赖的成对交互）各自编码不同的归纳偏置，且自诞生以来在数学形式上保持分离。作者认为这种分裂并非源于信号处理方式的本质差异，而是对同一底层数学对象——可学习积分变换——的不完整视角。因此，动机是揭示并利用这一统一数学对象，构建一个能涵盖并超越现有架构的通用框架。

Method: 1. **统一框架**：将卷积、自注意力和自回归循环统一为积分变换形式 $\mathcal{F}(x)(p) = \int \kappa(p, q, x(p), x(q)) \cdot x(q) \, dq$，其中核函数 $\kappa$ 依赖位置和特征。核函数由一个小型MLP实现，从数据中学习交互模式。
2. **特例推导**：证明在适当参数化下，该框架可退化为卷积（核仅依赖位置差）、自注意力/多头自注意力（核由softmax内积构成）、以及LSTM/GRU/S4/Mamba等循环架构。
3. **通用逼近性**：证明ITNet是连续算子的通用逼近器。
4. **实用化技术**：提出三项关键技术——**分块核融合**（tiled kernel fusion）、**重要性加权蒙特卡洛积分**、以及**可学习的低秩分解**，以实现高效可扩展计算。
5. **实验验证**：在ImageNet-1K（图像分类）、GLUE（自然语言理解）、ModelNet40（3D点云）、VQA v2和NLVR2（视觉问答）等跨模态任务上，使用共享算子加轻量模态专用编码器的单一ITNet架构进行评估。

Result: 单一ITNet架构在ImageNet-1K、GLUE、ModelNet40、VQA v2和NLVR2等任务上匹配或超越各领域的专用基线模型。实验表明，一个统一的、从数据中学习的交互机制，能够同时恢复卷积网络（局部性）、Transformer（内容依赖注意力）和循环网络（序列记忆）三类架构的行为，验证了积分变换作为统一数学基础的有效性。

Conclusion: 卷积网络、循环网络和Transformer并非本质不同的架构，而是同一可学习积分变换在不同参数化下的特例。ITNet通过一个由MLP实现的可学习核函数统一了三大架构家族，在理论上具备通用算子逼近能力，在实践中借助三项高效计算技术可扩展至大规模任务，并在多个跨模态基准上验证了统一架构的竞争力。这为未来模型设计提供了"从数据中学习最优交互模式"的新范式。

Abstract: Convolutional networks, recurrent networks, and transformers each encode different inductive biases -- locality, sequential memory, and content-dependent pairwise interaction -- and have remained mathematically distinct since their inception. We show that this fragmentation reflects not a fundamental diversity in how signals should be processed, but rather incomplete views of a single underlying mathematical object: a learnable integral transform. We introduce the Integral Transform Network (ITNet), a unified architecture built around a learnable kernel that depends jointly on positions and features. This kernel is implemented as a small neural network, specifically an MLP, that models pairwise interactions, enabling the model to adapt its behavior from data. We show that convolution, self-attention (including multi-head), and autoregressive recurrence (including LSTM, GRU, S4, and Mamba) arise as special cases under appropriate parameterizations, and that ITNet is a universal approximator of continuous operators. To make this practical, we develop tiled kernel fusion, importance-weighted Monte Carlo integration, and learned low-rank factorization, enabling efficient and scalable computation. A single ITNet architecture with a shared operator and lightweight modality-specific encoders matches or exceeds specialized baselines on ImageNet-1K , GLUE, ModelNet40, VQA\,v2 and NLVR2. The results demonstrate that a single learned interaction mechanism can recover the behavior of all three architectural families from data.

</details>


### [190] [Uncertainty Decomposition for Clarification Seeking in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19559)
*Gregory Matsnev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于提示的简单分解方法，将LLM智能体的动作置信度与请求不确定性分离，使其在任务规格模糊时能主动寻求澄清。在两类新基准上的实验表明，该方法在五个LLM骨干上一致优于现有方法，澄清F1最高提升73%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有认知不确定性/偶然不确定性框架不适用于交互式LLM智能体，业界呼吁设计可分解、可通信且能感知欠规格化的不确定性表示，以解锁主动澄清、共享心智模型等新能力。然而，黑盒API、延迟约束、缺乏标注轨迹等部署限制排除了基于logprob、多重采样和训练的方法，使得基于提示的估计成为唯一可行的方案。

Method: 提出一种基于提示的分解方法，将动作置信度（action confidence）与请求不确定性（request uncertainty）分离解耦，使智能体在任务规格模糊时能主动发出澄清请求。为进行评估，构建了两个澄清增强基准（WebShop-Clarification和ALFWorld-Clarification），其中50%的任务被刻意欠规格化。在五个LLM骨干上系统对比了所提方法与ReAct+UE及Uncertainty-Aware Memory（UAM）。

Result: 在五个LLM骨干上平均：所提方法在ALFWorld-Clarification上的澄清F1较ReAct+UE提升73%，较UAM提升36%；在WebShop-Clarification上所有骨干均领先，在ALFWorld-Clarification上五个骨干中有四个领先，表明增益具有跨模型泛化性。

Conclusion: 基于提示的动作置信度与请求不确定性分解方法能有效赋能LLM智能体在模糊场景下主动澄清，其效果在不同LLM骨干上具有稳健的泛化能力，为受限部署条件下的不确定性通信提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Recent position papers argue that the classical aleatoric/epistemic uncertainty framework is insufficient for interactive large language model (LLM) agents and call for underspecification-aware, decomposed, and communicable uncertainty representations that can unlock new agent capabilities such as proactive clarification seeking and shared mental-model building. Practical deployment constraints -- black-box APIs, interactive latency budgets, and the absence of labeled trajectories -- rule out logprob-based, multi-sampling, and training-based methods, leaving prompt-based estimation as the most viable family for surfacing such signals at deployment time. We answer this call with a simple prompt-based decomposition that separates action confidence from request uncertainty (u), enabling the agent to ask for clarification when the task specification is ambiguous. To evaluate it, we introduce two clarification-augmented benchmarks (WebShop-Clarification and ALFWorld-Clarification) in which 50% of tasks are deliberately underspecified, and systematically compare the proposed decomposition against ReAct+UE and Uncertainty-Aware Memory (UAM) across five LLM backbones (GPT-5.1, DeepSeek-v3.2-exp, GLM-4.7, Qwen3.5-35B, GPT-OSS-120B) on these variants together with the standard WebShop, ALFWorld, and REAL benchmarks for fault detection. Averaged across the five backbones, the proposed decomposition improves clarification F1 on ALFWorld-Clarification by 73% over ReAct+UE and by 36% over UAM, and leads clarification F1 on every backbone on WebShop-Clarification and on four of five backbones on ALFWorld-Clarification, indicating that the gains generalize beyond a single LLM.

</details>


### [191] [Analyzing the Narration Gap in LLM-Solver Loops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19588)
*Zunchen Huang,Songgaojun Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM与形式化求解器（SAT/SMT）混合推理管道中的"叙述"环节，发现即使求解器本身给出可靠判定，攻击者仍可通过提示注入在叙述阶段颠覆最终结论，且现有缓解措施无法彻底消除该漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化工具（SAT/SMT求解器）越来越多地嵌入LLM推理管道以保证安全关键问题的答案可靠性，但现有工作仅关注"形式化"和"判定"两个环节，忽视了将求解器输出转化为用户可读答案的"叙述"环节。叙述环节可能破坏求解器提供的可靠性保证，这一问题尚未被系统研究。

Method: 作者首先将LLM-求解器交互循环建模为一个可验证的判定过程（verified decision procedure），从而在形式上刻画叙述环节的安全边界。随后，对五个开源模型进行提示注入（prompt injection）实验评估，引入"证书门控"（certificate gating）机制检验其对求解器判定的保护效果，并测试对抗性攻击下不同措辞和输出通道的鲁棒性。最后研究了强化提示（hardened prompt）的缓解效果及其在自适应攻击下的局限性。

Result: 实验表明：证书门控能够有效保护求解器判定的可靠性；但攻击者可通过变换措辞和输出通道，使LLM在叙述阶段输出与求解器原始结论相反的答案。强化提示可显著降低注入成功率，但无法完全消除漏洞，且在自适应攻击下仍然脆弱。

Conclusion: 在LLM-求解器混合管道中，鲁棒性无法端到端地传递到用户最终阅读的答案。叙述环节构成了整个管道的薄弱环节，需要更多的研究关注以确保形式化推理在实际部署中的端到端可靠性。

Abstract: Formal tools such as SAT and SMT solvers are increasingly embedded in language model reasoning pipelines when a safety or security critical question can be formulated in logic. Unlike chain of thought whose steps are sampled from the model distribution without formal guarantee, a solver produces a sound and independently verifiable answer. However, the soundness guarantee can be lost in the interaction between the solver and the model. The hybrid pipeline has three components: formalizing the question, deciding it, and narrating the result. Prior work has studied the formalization and decision, but not narration, which is the step that turns a formal tool's output into the user answer. To fill the narration gap, we first model the LLM-solver loop as a verified decision procedure. We further evaluate five open-sourced models under prompt injection, and we find certificate gating makes the solver verdict sound, while an adversary can invert a verified conclusion across phrasings and channels. We study the mitigation through hardened prompt that reduces injection significantly but cannot eliminate it and still suffers under adaptive attack. Combining the formal analysis and empirical studies, we show in the LLM-solver loop, robustness does not reach to the answer that the user finally reads.

</details>


### [192] [Configurable Clinical Information Extraction with Agentic RAG: What Works, What Breaks, and Why](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19602)
*Osman Alperen Çinar-Koraş,Marie Bauer,Sameh Khattab,Merlin Engelke,Moon Kim,Stephan Settelmeier,Shigeyasu Sugawara,Fabian Freisleben,Felix Nensa,Jens Kleesiek*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文介绍了在德国埃森大学医学院部署的ACIE（Agentic Clinical Information Extraction）系统，这是一个本地化的智能体RAG流水线，能够跨越数百份异构文档进行推理，每项回答均附源文引用供临床核验。在包含7,326次判断的回顾性淋巴瘤注册研究中，临床医生对96.5%的提取结果予以接受。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 患者上下文涵盖数百份异构文档和数千个结构化数据点，但AI系统检索与分诊所需的文档级元数据缺失或不完整。标准RAG在时序推理、跨文档依赖和元数据缺失方面表现失效，因此需要一种能对完整患者上下文进行推理并提供可溯源引用的新方案。

Method: 部署名为ACIE的本地化（on-premise）智能体RAG流水线。该系统对完整患者上下文进行推理，并将每个回答锚定在源文段落上，供临床医生验证。通过与一项独立的回顾性淋巴瘤注册研究配合，由核医学医师逐条核实每个提取值与其引用来源。

Result: 在7,326次判断中，临床医生接受率为96.5%，各类型提取的接受率从80%到99%不等。论文同时量化了元数据缺口，并追溯了由此驱动的架构决策。

Conclusion: ACIE系统有效弥补了临床数据中元数据缺失带来的检索与提取困难，高接受率表明其提取质量和可溯源性获得了临床医生的认可，为大规模临床信息提取提供了可靠的技术路径。

Abstract: Patient contexts span hundreds of heterogeneous documents and thousands of structured data points, yet the document-level metadata that AI systems need for retrieval and triage is absent or incomplete. Standard retrieval-augmented generation fails on this data, mishandling temporal reasoning, cross-document dependencies, and missing metadata. We deploy ACIE (Agentic Clinical Information Extraction) at University Medicine Essen: an on-premise agentic RAG pipeline that reasons over complete patient contexts and grounds every answer in source passages for clinician verification. We quantify the metadata gap, trace the architectural decisions it shaped, and evaluate extraction alongside an independent retrospective lymphoma registry study, in which nuclear-medicine physicians verify every extracted value against its cited sources. Across 7,326 judgments, clinicians accepted 96.5\% of extractions, with per-type acceptance ranging from 80\% to 99\%.

</details>


### [193] [Which Pairs to Compare for LLM Post-Training?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19607)
*Jiangze Han,Vineet Goyal,Will Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于偏好的语言模型后训练中，如何在有限标注预算下选择最具信息量的比较对。作者将其形式化为采样设计问题，在DPO框架下推导出后训练最优性差距的匹配上下界，揭示了比较选择通过一个依赖设计的Fisher信息矩阵影响下游策略性能，并据此提出了预算约束下的显式优化准则和实用采样设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类偏好标注成本远高于生成额外补全文本的成本，但现有方法通常为每个提示仅生成少量补全并全部标注。核心问题是：给定固定标注预算，是生成少量补全后全部标注，还是生成大量补全后仅标注最有信息量的比较对？本文试图回答"哪些比较对应被选择用于偏好后训练"这一根本性问题。

Method: 作者将比较筛选问题形式化为采样设计（sampling-design）问题，在Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) 框架下进行理论分析。核心推导包括：(1) 建立DPO训练后策略最优性差距的上下界；(2) 证明比较选择通过一个与设计相关的信息矩阵（design-dependent information matrix）影响下游性能，该矩阵连接标注分配与参数估计误差及策略次优性；(3) 基于此推导出预算约束下比较筛选的显式优化准则；(4) 据此设计实用的采样策略，从大规模生成的补全池中选取信息量最大的比较对。

Result: 理论上，给出了DPO训练策略在后训练最优性差距上的匹配上下界，揭示了信息矩阵的核心作用。实验上，在合成场景和语言模型后训练基准测试中，所提出的设计相比常用的比较选择启发式方法（如随机选择）一致地提升了样本效率。

Conclusion: 在偏好后训练中，明智地选择哪些比较对进行标注比简单增加标注数量更为关键。通过将比较筛选视为采样设计问题，可以显著提升固定标注预算下的策略学习效率。该框架为预算约束下的偏好数据收集提供了理论指导和实用方案。

Abstract: Preference-based post-training has become a central paradigm for aligning language models. A common data-collection strategy is to generate a small set of completions for each prompt and label the resulting comparison pairs. However, human preference labels are often much more expensive than generating additional completions, suggesting a different use of the same labeling budget: generate a larger pool of completions, but label only the most informative comparison pairs. This paper studies which pairs should be compared in preference-based post-training. We formulate comparison curation as a sampling-design problem and evaluate designs by the quality of the final policy under the preference-based post-training objective. We instantiate this framework for Direct Preference Optimization (DPO), analyzing how the choice of labeled pairs propagates through DPO training to downstream policy performance. Our main results provide matching upper and lower bounds on the post-training optimality gap of the DPO-trained policy. The bounds show that comparison selection affects downstream performance through a single design-dependent information matrix, which links label allocation to parameter estimation error and policy suboptimality. This yields an explicit optimization criterion for budgeted comparison curation and motivates practical sampling designs for selecting informative pairs from large generated completion pools. Experiments on synthetic settings and language-model post-training benchmarks show that the proposed designs consistently improve sample efficiency over common comparison-selection heuristics.

</details>


### [194] [Toten: Knowledge-Based Ontological Tokenization Of Physical Quantities And Technical Notation In Brazilian Portuguese](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19626)
*Antonio de Sousa Leitão Filho; Allan Kardec Duailibe Barros Filho; Fabrício Saul Lima; Selby Mykael Lima dos Santos; Rejani Bandeira Vieira Sousa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TOTEN是一个基于知识本体的分词框架，利用工程实体本体(OEE)进行声明式分类，替代统计式BPE分词，解决了BPE对物理量、数字、单位和符号表达式碎片化的问题。通过与Pint、Unicode字符数据库和RSLP三个外部预言机的确定性耦合，TOTEN在本体原子性、量纲等价性、排版鲁棒性和数值重构四个维度上显著优于八种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Byte-Pair Encoding (BPE)等统计分词方法虽然在词汇压缩方面高效，但语义上对结构化技术实体"盲目"，会将物理量、数字、单位和符号表达式碎片化为任意子词。这严重损害了下游工程和科学NLP任务对精确语义理解的需求。

Method: 提出TOTEN框架，形式化为三元组⟨O, classify, {inst_τ}⟩：(1) O——工程实体本体(OEE)，汇集类型、结构原则、组合关系和可保持不变性；(2) classify——将原始文本映射到类型化区域的分类函数；(3) {inst_τ}——实例化器族，产生自描述的结构化表示。系统与三个外部预言机确定性耦合：Pint(量纲分析)、Unicode字符数据库(排版)、RSLP(葡萄牙语形态学)，从而获得鲁棒性。

Result: 在内部基准EngQuant(N=800)和四个巴西葡萄牙语外部语料库(N=1771)上评估。TOTEN在所有对比中实现单位本体原子性，外部语料数值重构达0.775-0.904(最佳基线Quantulum3仅0.627-0.703)，EngQuant上为0.780 vs 0.340。差异经McNemar检验及Holm校正具有统计显著性。内部与外部排名的Spearman相关性证实了对照基准的并发效度。量纲等价性与Pint达到统计平齐。

Conclusion: 基于知识本体的分词框架TOTEN通过声明式分类和外部预言机耦合，在工程和科学文本的结构化实体识别与重构方面显著优于统计式方法，验证了将领域本体嵌入分词底层的可行性与优势。

Abstract: Byte-Pair Encoding tokenization is statistically efficient for vocabulary compression, but semantically blind to structured technical entities, fragmenting physical quantities, numbers, units, and symbolic expressions into lexically arbitrary subwords. We present TOTEN, a knowledge-based ontological tokenization framework that replaces statistical derivation with declarative classification grounded in a formal ontology of engineering entities (OEE). We formalize TOTEN as the triple <O, classify, {inst_tau}>: the ontology gathers types, structural principles, composition relations, and preservable invariants; the classification function maps raw text into typed regions; and the instantiator family yields a self-descriptive structured representation. Robustness derives from deterministic coupling with three external oracles: Pint (dimensional), Unicode Character Database (typographic), and RSLP (Portuguese morphology). Intrinsic evaluation covers four properties verifiable by construction -- ontological atomicity, dimensional equivalence, typographic robustness, and numerical reconstruction -- over an internal, physically validated benchmark (EngQuant, N=800) and four Brazilian Portuguese external corpora (N=1771 eligible cases). We also report detection recall, distinguishing coverage from conditional atomicity. Against eight state-of-the-art baselines, TOTEN achieves unit ontological atomicity in all contrasts and numerical reconstruction of 0.775-0.904 on external corpora, vs. 0.627-0.703 for the best baseline (Quantulum3); on EngQuant, 0.780 vs. 0.340. Differences are statistically significant (McNemar with Holm correction). Spearman correlation between internal and external rankings confirms concurrent validity of the control benchmark. Dimensional equivalence shows statistical parity with Pint, the oracle from which the system inherits dimensional authority.

</details>


### [195] [AI4SE and SE4AI Exploration: A Decade Looking Back and Forward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19630)
*H. Sinan Bank,Daniel R. Herber,Thomas Bradley*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文回顾了AI与系统工程交叉领域的发展历程，将其分为基础、应用和LLM拐点三个阶段，并通过人机一致性文献综述识别出五个关键研究空白，同时发布了AI4SE/SE4AI Explorer工具供读者交互探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: INCOSE INSIGHT 2020年3月特刊的成功催生了一个活跃的研究社群，但该领域仍存在未被充分解决的关键空白。作者旨在系统梳理AI与SE交叉领域的演进脉络，并借助人机协作的文献综述，定量评估领域内大量出版物的相关性，以识别研究空白并指导实践。

Method: 采用三阶段框架（基础期、应用期、LLM拐点期）对领域核心论文进行定性回顾；同时开展人机一致性文献综述，结合人类专家与六种AI模型，对1,712篇INCOSE INSIGHT文章和889篇SERC出版物进行相关性评估，并开发了AI4SE/SE4AI Explorer网络应用以共享数据和实现交互式比较。

Result: 识别出五个关键研究空白；提供了AI采纳、保障及SE劳动力转型方面的实践指导；通过Web应用公开了人机一致性数据，使读者可对照自身判断与人类及AI评分者的相关性评估。

Conclusion: AI与SE交叉领域经历了从基础到应用再到LLM驱动的范式转变，虽已取得显著进展，但仍存在五个关键研究空白亟待填补。人机协作的文献综述方法本身也为大规模文献评估提供了可复用的范式。

Abstract: The March 2020 INCOSE INSIGHT special issue on AI and Systems Engineering (SE) became the most downloaded issue in the publication's history and launched a research community that now draws over 250 registrants to its annual workshop. In this article, we trace the progress in AI and SE across three phases (labeled here foundational, applied, and LLM inflection) based on the authors' reading of the field's core papers, and describe our opinions of where the community has converged and where critical gaps remain. Separately, a human-AI agreement literature review leveraging both human expertise and six AI models was performed to assess the relevance of 1,712 INCOSE INSIGHT articles and 889 SERC publications. The results identify five critical research gaps and offer guidance for practitioners navigating AI adoption, assurance, and workforce transformation in SE. We share the agreement data and the AI4SE/SE4AI Explorer web application so readers can compare their own relevance judgments with the human and AI raters.

</details>


### [196] [BrainG3N: A Dual-Purpose Tokenizer for Controllable 3D Brain MRI Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19651)
*Max Van Puyvelde,Ibrahim Gulluk,Wim Van Criekinge,Olivier Gevaert*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于全三维掩码自编码器（MAE）的tokenizer，用于3D脑MRI的潜在扩散模型。通过解耦编码器与解码器：冻结的3D MAE编码器生成富含临床信息的嵌入，专用CNN解码器从嵌入的线性投影中重建体素。该编码器在35,309个多模态多中心数据上预训练后，在23项线性探测任务中21项超越或匹配SOTA，且基于该嵌入的条件扩散Transformer支持六变量条件生成和患者纵向预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在扩散模型的tokenizer存在两难困境：编码器嵌入需保留临床信息以支撑下游任务，解码器需重建解剖学上保真的体积，而现有重建驱动的tokenizer往往以牺牲前者为代价实现后者。

Method: 提出全体积3D MAE tokenizer，将编码器与解码器解耦：(1) 冻结的3D MAE编码器在35,309个3D脑MRI体积（18个公开队列、4种模态、10种疾病、200+采集点）上预训练，产生临床信息丰富的嵌入；(2) 专用CNN解码器从这些嵌入的线性投影中重建体素；(3) 基于该嵌入训练条件扩散Transformer（DiT），实现六变量条件生成和患者特异性纵向预测。

Result: 在23任务线性探测基准上，该编码器在21/23任务上超越或匹配BrainIAC、BrainSegFounder、MedicalNet等SOTA模型；条件DiT成功支持六变量条件生成和纵向预测。

Conclusion: 建立了一个统一的3D脑MRI嵌入空间，同时支撑下游临床任务和可控生成，解决了tokenizer中临床信息保留与重建保真度之间的权衡问题。

Abstract: Three-dimensional (3D) brain MRI is central to clinical neurology and neuro-oncology, where generative models could augment under-represented cohorts, simulate disease trajectories, and support privacy-preserving data sharing. Latent diffusion has been the go-to solution for modeling imaging data, but it places two competing demands on the tokenizer: encoder embeddings must retain the clinical information that downstream tasks act on, and the decoder must reconstruct anatomically faithful volumes. Existing reconstruction-driven tokenizers achieve the second at the expense of the first. To address this, we introduce a fully volumetric masked-autoencoder (MAE) based tokenizer for 3D brain MRI latent diffusion, decoupling encoder and decoder: a frozen 3D MAE encoder produces clinically informative embeddings, while a dedicated CNN decoder reconstructs voxels from a linear projection of those embeddings. We pretrain the encoder on 35,309 volumes from 18 public cohorts spanning four modalities, ten disease categories, and 200+ acquisition sites, and demonstrate its dual utility in two settings. First, on a 23-task linear-probing benchmark, the encoder outperforms or matches SOTA models (i.e., BrainIAC, BrainSegFounder, and MedicalNet) on 21 of 23 tasks. Second, a conditional diffusion transformer (DiT) trained on these clinically informative embeddings supports both conditional generation across six variables and patient-specific longitudinal forecasting. Together these results establish a single 3D brain-MRI embedding space capable of both downstream clinical tasks and controllable generation.

</details>


### [197] [Denoising Implicit Feedback for Cold-start Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19658)
*Gaode Chen,Shicheng Wang,Shikun Li,Rui Huang,Xinghua Zhang,Yunze Luo,Shipeng Li,Shiming Ge,Ruina Sun,Yinjie Jiang,Jun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对推荐系统中隐式反馈噪声与物品冷启动问题的耦合，本文提出模型无关的去噪方法DIF。其核心思想是利用内容相似的暖物品推断伪标签，通过内容相似度对伪标签置信度建模并聚合，再结合相对熵和物品冷启动状态估计标签不确定性，从而自适应地利用伪标签修正噪声标签。该方法已在快手落地部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冷物品由于缺乏交互数据，更容易受到点击诱饵、位置偏差等隐式反馈噪声的影响。然而现有去噪方法（如基于损失值的选择或重加权）依赖启发式模式，自适应性差，在冷启动场景下效果不佳，且研究者普遍忽视了针对冷物品的隐式反馈去噪问题。

Method: 提出DIF（Denoising Implicit Feedback）方法：(1) 利用用户对内容的偏好稳定性，通过内容相似的暖物品推断用户是否对冷物品感兴趣的伪标签；(2) 基于冷物品与暖物品之间的内容相似度建模伪标签置信度，并对每个样本聚合多个伪标签；(3) 综合考虑样本的相对熵和物品的冷启动状态，显式估计噪声标签的不确定性，在样本级别自适应地利用伪标签纠正噪声标签。方法具有模型无关性。

Result: 理论分析证明该方法的合理性；在真实世界数据集上的大量实验验证了DIF的优越性；已在十亿用户规模的短视频应用快手落地部署，在冷启动场景下显著提升了多项商业指标。

Conclusion: DIF有效解决了冷物品隐式反馈去噪这一被忽视的问题，通过内容感知的伪标签生成与不确定性引导的自适应校正机制，实现了冷启动场景下的推荐性能提升，兼具理论保证和工业实践价值。

Abstract: Implicit feedback is widely used in recommender systems due to its accessibility and generality, yet it usually presents noisy samples (e.g., clickbait, position bias). Meanwhile, recommenders inevitably face the item cold-start problem due to the continuous influx of new items. We identify that cold items are more prone to noisy samples due to the aforementioned factors, and researchers often overlook the significance of denoising implicit feedback for cold items. Previous denoising studies usually identify noisy samples based on heuristic patterns, such as higher loss values, and mitigate noise through sample selection or re-weighting. However, these methods have limited adaptability and are ineffective in cold-start scenarios. To achieve denoising implicit feedback for cold-start recommendation, we propose a model-agnostic denoising method called DIF. First, user preferences for content remain stable, which allows us to infer pseudo-labels indicating whether a user is interested in a cold item through content-similar warm items. Furthermore, to improve pseudo-label accuracy, we model the confidence of pseudo-labels based on the content similarity between the cold item and warm items, and then aggregate multiple pseudo-labels for each sample. Finally, we explicitly estimate the uncertainty of the noisy sample label by considering its relative entropy and the cold-start status of the item, which adaptively guides the role of pseudo-labels to correct the noisy labels at the sample level. DIF's superiority is supported by both theoretical justification and extensive experiments on real-world datasets. The method has been deployed on a billion-user scale short video application Kuaishou and has significantly improved various commercial metrics within cold-start scenarios.

</details>


### [198] [Exit-and-Join Dynamics for Decentralized Coalition Formation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19683)
*Quanyan Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究联盟形成作为一种由单方面退出-加入决策驱动的去中心化动态过程。代理人使用Aumann-Drèze值评估局部移动，将合作收益分配与非合作最优响应行为联系起来，建立均衡特征，识别动力学存在标量Lyapunov或精确势函数的条件，并分析转换与接受成本对局部稳定性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统合作博弈中联盟形成通常依赖全局协商与特征函数，但现实中代理人常基于当前所处联盟的局部收益评估做出去中心化决策。作者意在填补合作收益分配与非合作动态行为之间的理论鸿沟，构建一个微观基础更符合实际分散决策情境的联盟形成模型。

Method: 将联盟形成建模为代理人单方面退出-加入驱动的去中心化动态系统。每个代理人使用Aumann-Drèze值（仅依据所在联盟内部进行收益分配）评估局部移动的个体收益。动态的终端划分被定义为无可容许且个体有利的退出-加入偏离的联盟结构。作者推导均衡的刻画条件，寻找动能标量Lyapunov函数或精确势函数表示的条件，并引入转换成本与接受成本参数分析其对局部稳定性的作用，最后通过数值实验验证有限时间稳定化、成本敏感性以及凸博弈基准测试。

Result: 得到终端划分恰好对应于无个体有利偏离的联盟结构的均衡刻画；识别出动力学存在标量Lyapunov函数或精确势函数的充分条件；揭示了转换成本和接受成本对局部稳定性的塑造机制；数值实验展示了有限时间收敛、成本参数敏感性以及在凸博弈特例下模型的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过Aumann-Drèze值将合作收益分配与非合作最优响应动态联系起来，为去中心化联盟形成提供了严格的理论框架。该模型能够解释局部激励如何驱动全局联盟结构的涌现与稳定，切换与接受成本是影响均衡收敛的关键因素，为理解现实中的分散联盟形成提供了可操作的均衡分析与稳定性判据。

Abstract: This paper studies coalition formation as a decentralized dynamical process driven by unilateral exit-and-join decisions. Agents evaluate local moves using the Aumann-Dreze value, so payoffs are computed within the agent's current coalition rather than through a globally negotiated coalition structure. The resulting model links cooperative payoff allocation with noncooperative best-response behavior: a terminal partition is precisely a coalition structure with no admissible, individually profitable exit-and-join deviation. We establish equilibrium characterizations, identify conditions under which the dynamics admit scalar Lyapunov or exact-potential representations, and analyze how switching and acceptance costs shape local stability. Numerical experiments test finite-time stabilization, cost sensitivity, and a special convex-game benchmark.

</details>


### [199] [Beyond Static Leaderboards: Predictive Validity for the Evaluation of LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19704)
*Dhaval C. Patel,Kaoutar El Maghraoui,Shuxin Lin,Yusheng Li,Tianjun Feng,Chun-Yi Tsai,Yihan Sun,Wei Alexander Xin,Akshat Bhandari,Tanisha Rathod,Aaron Fan,Sanskruti Vijay Shejwal,Tomas Pasiecznik,Sagar Chethan Kumar,Tanmay Agarwal,Rohith Kanathur,Sam Colman,Amaan Sheikh,Dev Bahl,Ann Li,Krish Veera,Alimurtaza Mustafa Merchant,Shambhawi Baswaraj Bhure,Sajal Kumar Goyla,Chengrui Li,Kirthana Natarajan,Rui Li,Thomas Ajai,Rujing Li,Vivek G. Iyer,Sanjaii Vijayakumar,Yitong Bai,Ayal Yakobe,Darief Maes,Yassine Jebbouri,Tianyang Xu,Thai Quoc On,Vera Mazeeva,Winston Li,Yuval Shemla,Yeshitha Bhuvanesh,Rushin Bhatt,Siddharth Chethan Gowda,Alisha Vinod,Caroline Cahill,Shriya Aishani Rachakonda,Yunfeng Chen,Aryaman Agrawal,Aman Upganlawar,Mao Le Jonathan Ang,Yubin Sally Go,Madhav Rajkondawar,Yang-Jung Chen,Trisha Maturi,Ananya Kapoor,Andrew Li,Shrey Arora,Mana Abbaszadeh,Shen Li,Charles Xu,Byeolah Kwon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文汇总了迄今最大规模的MCP工业智能体基准测试深度研究（14项并行实现研究），指出聚合评分排行榜系统性低估了部署评估需求，并提出基于预测效度（样本内与样本外排名相关性）的排名配置方案，构建了十二层测量装置，并通过三个可证伪的分布外准则进行操作性定义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前智能体基准测试虽快速增长，但单一基准仅覆盖部署中四到五个维度，聚合评分排行榜在分布外场景下排名不稳定，无法有效指导实际部署决策。需要更全面的评估框架来捕捉部署相关维度。

Method: (1) 汇总14项并行实现研究，涵盖新资产类别、多模态视觉扩展、替代编排、检索策略、推理模式、基础设施优化及评估方法论探索；(2) 与7个先前基准测试进行整合分析；(3) 提出以预测效度（样本内-样本外排名相关性）替代样本内均值作为排名依据；(4) 构建十二层测量装置覆盖部署相关维度；(5) 提出三个带显式阈值的可证伪分布外准则进行操作化验证。

Result: (1) 聚合评分排行榜存在系统性不足，排名在分布外场景下无法迁移；(2) 近期公开到隐藏竞赛的回顾性分析提供了排名不稳定的直接实证；(3) 十二层测量装置能够暴露HELM及其智能体时代后继者所压缩的部署相关维度；(4) 现有证据部分支持该框架，但仍不够充分。

Conclusion: 智能体基准测试应从聚合评分排名转向基于预测效度的评估范式。论文提出了预注册试点设计和下一代智能体基准测试应报告的领域级愿景，呼吁构建更全面的、面向部署的评估体系。

Abstract: Agent benchmarks are growing fast, but no single benchmark touches more than four or five of the dimensions that deployment exposes. This paper aggregates the largest coordinated deep-dive of one MCP-based industrial-agent benchmark to date: fourteen parallel implementation studies covering new asset classes (including a multi-modal visual extension), alternative orchestrations, retrieval strategies, reasoning modes, infrastructure optimizations, and evaluation-methodology probes. Consolidating those studies with seven prior agent benchmarks, we argue that aggregate-score leaderboards systematically underspecify deployed-agent evaluation. Rankings derived from aggregate scores do not transfer to out-of-distribution settings; recent public-to-hidden competition retrospectives provide direct empirical evidence of this rank instability. We propose ranking configurations by predictive validity, the correlation between in-sample and out-of-sample rank, rather than in-sample mean, and report a twelve-tier measurement apparatus that exposes the deployment-relevant dimensions HELM and its agent-era successors collapse. The position is operationalized through three falsifiable out-of-distribution criteria with explicit thresholds; existing evidence partly supports it but is too thin to confirm. We close with a pre-registered pilot design and a field-level vision for what the next generation of agentic benchmarks should report.

</details>


### [200] [GLARE: A Natural Language Interface for Querying Global Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19735)
*Bhavan Vasu,Rajesh Mangannavar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于LLM的交互式界面，将自然语言问题转化为结构化SQL查询，对黑盒图像分类器的全局解释数据进行灵活聚合，输出统计增强的自然语言回答与可视化，显著提升全局解释在人类中心可解释AI中的可访问性和可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局解释对于理解视觉模型跨数据集、跨类别和跨决策场景的行为至关重要，但其复杂且单一的表现形式阻碍了实际探索。用户通常需要针对特定问题的定向回答，而非静态的解释产物。为此，需要一种更灵活、更易用的全局解释查询方式。

Method: 构建以LLM为核心中介的交互式界面：LLM将用户的自然语言问题翻译为结构化SQL查询，基于局部解释数据（如SHAP、LIME等）进行灵活聚合，无需用户接触底层表示。每次查询返回统计增强的自然语言回答，并辅以局部解释和意图对齐的可视化。系统评估涵盖意图理解、查询映射准确率、对新查询和数据集的泛化能力，以及对语言错误的鲁棒性。

Result: LLM介导的查询方式在意图解释准确率、SQL查询映射精度、新查询与数据集泛化能力以及语言错误鲁棒性方面均表现良好，有效提升了全局解释的可访问性和可用性。

Conclusion: LLM作为自然语言与结构化解释数据之间的中介，能够显著降低用户探索全局解释的门槛，为人类中心的可解释AI（XAI）提供了一种更实用、更灵活的交互范式。

Abstract: While global explanations are crucial for understanding vision models across datasets, classes, and decision contexts, their complex and monolithic nature often hinders practical exploration. Because users typically seek targeted answers to specific questions rather than static artifacts, we present an LLM-based interactive interface that provides natural language access to global explanations for black-box image classifiers. The system's core LLM acts as a mediator, translating natural language questions into structured SQL queries over local explanation data. This enables flexible aggregation without exposing users to low-level representations. For each query, the interface outputs statistics-augmented natural language responses, supporting local explanations, and intent-aligned visualizations. We evaluate the system on intent interpretation, query mapping accuracy, generalization to novel queries and datasets, and robustness to linguistic errors. Our results demonstrate that LLM-mediated querying substantially improves the accessibility and usability of global explanations for human-centered XAI.

</details>


### [201] [Interpreting Neural Combinatorial Optimization via Evolving Programmatic Bottlenecks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19741)
*Haocheng Duan,Yuxin Guo,Jieyi Bi,Anqi Xie,Sirui Li,Yining Ma,Cathy Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EPB 是首个将黑箱神经组合优化模型蒸馏为可解释程序组合的框架，通过LLM自动演化程序库，结合混合文本-数值梯度下降和动态容量调整，使得蒸馏后的程序组合在性能上与原模型相当，并揭示了NCO行为的阶段性特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经组合优化（NCO）虽性能强劲，但其黑箱特性严重阻碍了部署与科学诊断。现有可解释性工具（如概念瓶颈模型CBM）无法适配NCO，因为NCO的决策是动态的、状态依赖的，且缺乏恰当的概念词汇定义。

Method: 提出Evolving Programmatic Bottlenecks (EPB)框架，分两阶段迭代：Block I固定程序库容量，引入混合文本-数值梯度下降——数值梯度用于更新学生路由器，文本梯度驱动LLM进行程序修订；Block II通过故障导向扩展和冗余剪枝动态调整程序库容量。核心思想是让每个程序的每步动作分布作为瓶颈，由LLM自主演化程序库。

Result: 在广泛实验中，蒸馏出的程序组合性能与原NCO模型高度匹配，同时EPB揭示了NCO行为随优化阶段变化，且可被近似为经典启发式变体的组合。

Conclusion: EPB为可解释NCO开辟了新路径，并具备作为解释序列决策模型通用工具的潜力，推动了可解释组合优化领域的发展。

Abstract: Neural Combinatorial Optimization (NCO) achieves strong performance, yet its black-box nature remains a key roadblock to deployment and scientific diagnosis. Standard interpretability tools, such as Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs), are ill-equipped for NCO, whose decisions are dynamic, state-dependent, and lack proper concept vocabulary definition. To close this gap, we introduce Evolving Programmatic Bottlenecks (EPB), to our knowledge, the first framework for interpreting NCO policies by distilling black-box NCO models into human-readable program portfolios. EPB employs an LLM to autonomously evolve a bank of programs, where each program's per-step action distribution serves as the bottleneck. EPB works through an iterative framework: Block I fixes program bank capacity and introduces a hybrid textual-numerical gradient descent scheme that couples numerical gradients for student router updates and textual gradients for LLM-based program revision; Block II dynamically adapts bank capacity via fault-targeted expansion and redundancy pruning. Extensive experiments demonstrate EPB's effectiveness and broad applicability, where the distilled program portfolios largely match original performance. EPB also reveals that NCO behavior shifts across optimization stages and can be approximated as a composition of classic heuristic variants. Our work advances interpretable NCO and establishes EPB as a promising tool for interpreting sequential decision-making models.

</details>


### [202] [A Comparative Study of Pretrained Transformer Models for Quranic ASR: Speech Representations, Label Formats, and Dataset Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19747)
*Nabil Mosharraf Hossain,Riasat Islam,Unaizah Obaidellah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了基于预训练Transformer模型（Wav2Vec2.0、HuBERT、XLS-R）在古兰经自动语音识别领域的微调策略，在870+小时数据集上通过消融实验，最优配置在EveryAyah子集上实现0.08的WER，较Citrinet基线提升约5个百分点，并将训练时间从140小时压缩至40小时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ASR模型在用户诵读的古兰经经文上词错误率（WER）较高，且未能完整覆盖古兰经全文语料，限制了辅助记忆工具和古兰经搜索引擎等应用的发展。

Method: 采用Wav2Vec2.0、HuBERT和XLS-R三种自监督预训练语音特征提取器，在过滤后超过870小时的专业与用户诵读数据集上对Transformer模型进行领域特定微调。通过系统的消融研究，考察了特征提取器选择、输出标签格式（有无变音符号）、训练策略及音频片段时长等因素对转录精度的影响。

Result: 最优配置在EveryAyah子集上WER为0.08，在EveryAyah+Tarteel组合设置上WER为0.11，相比Citrinet基线（WER=0.163）提升约5个百分点，同时训练时间从140小时降至40小时。无变音符号的阿拉伯文本微调效果最佳，Wav2Vec2-XLSR-53提供最强的整体表征。

Conclusion: 基于XLS-R的自监督表征迁移在古兰经ASR领域显著优于传统端到端模型；去除变音符号可简化标签空间从而提升识别精度。未来工作聚焦于提升数据集质量及开发音素感知模型，以提取更深层语音特征用于泰吉威德规则敏感应用。

Abstract: Quran Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) aims to convert Quranic recitation into text, enabling applications such as aided memorisation tools and Quranic search engines. However, existing ASR models often exhibit high Word Error Rates (WER) on user-recited verses and lack full coverage of the Quranic corpus. This paper presents a systematic empirical study of domain-specific fine-tuning of pretrained Transformer-based models for Quranic ASR, using advanced speech feature extraction methods: Wav2Vec2.0, HuBERT, and XLS-R. These models apply self-supervised learning by masking portions of input audio and using Transformer architectures to learn context-aware speech features. The pretrained models are fine-tuned on a filtered Quranic dataset exceeding 870 hours of professional and user recitations. Through comprehensive ablation studies across feature extractors, output label formats, training strategies, and clip durations, we identify the key factors that affect transcription accuracy in this domain. Our best-performing configuration achieves a WER of 0.08 on the EveryAyah subset and 0.11 on the combined EveryAyah+Tarteel setting, representing roughly a five-percentage-point gain over the Citrinet baseline (WER = 0.163) while reducing combined-model training time from 140 hours to 40 hours. Arabic text without diacritics yields the best fine-tuning results, and Wav2Vec2-XLSR-53 provides the strongest overall representation. Future work includes improving dataset quality and developing phoneme-aware models to extract deeper speech feature representations for Tajweed-sensitive applications.

</details>


### [203] [Benchmarking Agentic Review Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19749)
*Dang Nguyen,Wanqing Hao,Yanai Elazar,Chenhao Tan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文系统评估了三类AI审稿系统（两款开源、一款闭源及零样本基线）在六个LLM上的表现：通过引用/录用决策等外部信号检验审稿质量排序能力（最优达83.0%成对准确率），通过注入四类错误的扰动基准测错误检测召回率（最优71.6%，多模型联合达83.3%），并分析真实用户部署反馈（好评率1.44:1，主要抱怨假阳性和琐碎挑剔），表明AI审稿虽仍有提升空间，但已能较好追踪人类质量判断并捕获关键错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助研究给传统同行评审带来巨大压力，新兴的智能体式审稿系统应运而生，但缺乏系统性的评估方法来衡量这些AI审稿系统的实际表现。

Method: 首先利用ICLR/NeurIPS论文的引用次数和录用决策作为质量代理信号，测试AI审稿的成对排序准确率；其次构建扰动基准，向八类arXiv论文注入四种类型错误，测量各系统的检测召回率；最后在真实用户场景中部署OpenAIReview，收集投票与反馈。

Result: 成对准确率方面，OpenAIReview+GPT-5.5达83.0%；错误检测方面，单系统最高召回71.6%，六模型联合召回83.3%，表明不同模型检测不同错误；真实部署中好评差评比1.44:1，主要抱怨为假阳性与琐碎挑剔。

Conclusion: AI审稿系统虽尚未完善，但已能较好地追踪人类质量判断、捕获重要错误，并获得真实用户的正面反馈，未来通过更好的集成设计可进一步提升性能。

Abstract: A new class of agentic review systems are emerging as a remedy to the pressure placed on peer review systems by AI-assisted research, but it is unclear how they should be evaluated. We evaluate two open-source systems (OpenAIReview and coarse), one proprietary system (Reviewer3), and a zero-shot baseline, across six LLMs spanning frontier and efficient models. First, we study whether AI reviews on ICLR/NeurIPS papers track with papers' quality as approximated by external signals such as citations and acceptance decisions. Every system performs above chance in pairwise accuracy, and the best is OpenAIReview + GPT-5.5 at 83.0%. Second, to test whether systems can catch errors with known ground truth, we construct a perturbation benchmark that injects four categories of errors into papers across eight arXiv subject classes and measure detection recall. The strongest configuration (OpenAIReview + GPT-5.5) catches 71.6% of injected errors, leaving substantial room for improvement. The union of detections across six models reaches 83.3% recall, suggesting different models detect different errors and better harness design can potentially increase performance. Beyond these benchmarks, we study a public deployment of OpenAIReview with real users. Votes on its comments skew positive at 1.44 to 1, and the most common complaints are about false positives and minor nitpicks. Together, by evaluating full review systems backed by state-of-the-art models on real research papers, we show that while AI reviews still have room for improvement, they can already track human quality judgments well, catch important errors, and earn positive feedback from real users.

</details>


### [204] [Grounded Inference: Principles for Deterministically Encapsulated Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19753)
*Marty O'Neill*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了AI混合架构的四个基本原语和两个反模式，旨在为将生成式模型确定性地封装入传统计算系统提供基础框架，以降低集成风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式模型融入传统计算系统既带来巨大机遇，也伴随严重风险。尽管早期采用者已付出高昂代价，但业界仍缺乏降低AI集成风险的基础性框架。

Method: 作者定义了AI混合架构的四个特定原语（primitives），用于实现对概率模型的确定性封装（deterministic encapsulation）；同时归纳出业界广泛存在的两个总体反模式（anti-patterns），作为工程实践的警示。

Result: 该框架为生成式模型成功融入传统系统提供了方法论基础，并可为下一代生成式模型接口的设计提供指导。

Conclusion: 通过定义架构原语与反模式，本文为AI与传统系统的安全、可靠集成奠定了理论基础，有望推动生成式模型接口的标准化演进。

Abstract: The incorporation of generative models into traditional computational systems presents both enormous opportunity and tremendous peril. Although many early adopters have realized these perils at great expense, the field still requires foundational frameworks to de-risk incorporation of AI into traditional systems. This manuscript establishes this foundation through the definition of four specific primitives of AI blended architecture, designed to enable deterministic encapsulation of probabilistic models. It further establishes two overarching anti-patterns broadly represented across industry to serve as warnings for engineers in this field. This framework was designed to enable successful integration of AI into traditional systems while providing a foundation upon which generative model providers could build the next generation of generative model interfaces.

</details>


### [205] [Optimal Scheduling in a Question-Answering Forum of Knowledge Workers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19759)
*Rohit Negi,Mustafa Yilmaz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究了基于知识工作者的问答论坛调度问题，计算系统在保持稳定前提下的最大处理能力（容量），并设计了能达到该容量的调度策略，同时探讨了专家协作对系统容量的提升效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前在线问答论坛依赖志愿者参与，未来可能转向雇佣知识工作者（专家）的模式。在这种系统中，如何高效地将不同主题的请求分配给具有不同专业水平的专家，以最大化系统吞吐量并保持稳定，是一个关键的调度问题。

Method: 将请求-回答过程建模为排队系统，引入能够根据专家在不同主题上的专业水平进行任务分配的调度器。通过排队论分析，计算系统在稳定条件下可处理请求的容量上界，并设计达到该容量的调度算法。同时建模专家之间的协作机制，分析其对容量的影响。

Result: 给出了系统容量的理论计算结果，并提出了能够达到最大容量的调度器设计方案。证明了专家之间的协作可以进一步提升系统容量。

Conclusion: 通过合理的调度策略，基于知识工作者的问答系统可以最大化其请求处理能力；专家协作是提升系统效率的有效手段。

Abstract: As individuals turn to the Internet to find answers to questions they may have, several Question Answering (QA) forums have evolved, where users knowledgeable in certain topics can contribute their expertise to answering these requests for information. While these are currently volunteer based, we consider a future version employing knowledge workers who are experts in certain topics. In such a system, the request-answer processes forming the queuing system may utilize schedulers that assign requests in different topics to the experts in the forum, who may be able to answer them according to their expertise levels in different topics. With this model, we calculate the capacity of the system for handling the requests while keeping the system stable, and design schedulers that achieve capacity. We also investigate how collaboration between experts in answering requests can potentially increase capacity.

</details>


### [206] [Beyond Entropy: Learning from Token-Level Distributional Deviations for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19771)
*Xuanzhi Feng,Zhengyang Li,Zeyu Liu,Haoxi Li,Yuming Jiang,Bing Guo,Jingcai Guo,Jie Zhang,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对RLVR中熵坍缩与熵爆炸的两难困境，本文提出ICT框架，利用JS散度识别具有独特分布模式的token进行选择性更新，从理论上证明该方法能同时调控Shannon熵和二阶Rényi熵，从而稳定训练过程。实验在Qwen2.5系列模型上，仅更新top 10%独特token即可在七个基准上平均提升pass@4指标4.58%，最高提升14.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR在LLM推理训练中存在根本性的优化不稳定问题：对所有token进行均匀更新会导致熵坍缩（过早收敛到次优策略），而过度最大化Shannon熵又会引发熵爆炸（盲目探索导致不连贯的推理链）。现有方法在这两极之间缺乏精细的调控手段。

Method: 提出ICT（Independent Combinatorial Tokens）框架：(1) 将优化焦点从标量不确定性转向token logits的分布特性；(2) 利用token logits分布之间的Jensen-Shannon (JS) 散度，识别具有独特分布模式的token作为关键分支点；(3) 仅对这些关键token进行选择性更新；(4) 理论分析基于Shannon熵和二阶Rényi熵，证明该方法能同时降低整体分布不确定性（Shannon熵）并控制概率集中度（二阶Rényi熵），从而实现训练景观的稳定化。

Result: 在Qwen2.5（0.5B/1.5B/7B）模型上，仅更新top 10%独特token，在涵盖数学、常识和奥林匹克级别问题的七个基准上，相比GRPO、20-Entropy和STAPO基线，pass@4指标平均提升4.58%，最大提升达14.9%。

Conclusion: ICT框架通过分布层面的token选择机制，有效解决了RLVR中的熵调控困境，实现了训练稳定性和探索效率的双重提升。其核心洞察是：基于JS散度的token分布差异性识别，能够精准定位对探索至关重要的决策点，从而以最小的更新代价获得显著的性能增益。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has significantly advanced Large Language Model (LLM) reasoning; however, it faces a fundamental optimization instability: uniform token updates precipitate entropy collapse, leading to premature convergence to suboptimal strategies, whereas excessive Shannon Entropy maximization can cause entropy explosion, driving blind exploration toward incoherent reasoning chains. To resolve this dichotomy, we introduce the Independent Combinatorial Tokens (ICT) framework, which shifts the optimization focus from scalar uncertainty to the distributional properties of token logits. By leveraging the Jensen-Shannon (JS) divergence between token logits distributions, ICT identifies tokens with distinctive distributional patterns as critical branching points for guiding effective exploration in LLM reasoning. Our theoretical analysis, grounded in both Shannon and second-order Rényi entropy, proves that selectively updating on these tokens regulates policy concentration: it reduces the overall distribution uncertainty measured by Shannon entropy, while controlling probability concentration captured by second-order Rényi entropy. This dual effect prevents over-concentrated token generation from weakening exploration and effectively stabilizes the training landscape. Empirical results demonstrate that updating only the top 10% of unique tokens on Qwen2.5 (0.5B/1.5B/7B) models yields an average pass@4 improvement of 4.58%, with a maximum gain of 14.9%, over GRPO, 20-Entropy, and STAPO baselines across seven benchmarks spanning math, commonsense, and Olympiad-level problems.

</details>
